import {
  CamProfileId,
  InductionDressMode,
  PistonAlloy,
  RingApplication
} from '../types/engine';
import { ENGINE_FAMILIES_DOMESTIC_DIESEL } from './engineFamiliesDomesticDiesel';
import { ENGINE_FAMILIES_ASIA } from './engineFamiliesAsia';
import { ENGINE_FAMILIES_EURO } from './engineFamiliesEuro';

export type EngineBrand =
  | 'GM / Chevrolet'
  | 'Pontiac / Oldsmobile / Buick'
  | 'Ford'
  | 'Chrysler / Mopar'
  | 'Jeep / AMC'
  | 'Toyota / Lexus'
  | 'Honda / Acura'
  | 'Nissan / Infiniti'
  | 'Subaru'
  | 'Mazda'
  | 'Mitsubishi'
  | 'Hyundai / Kia / Genesis'
  | 'BMW / MINI'
  | 'Mercedes-Benz / AMG'
  | 'Volkswagen / Audi / Porsche'
  | 'Volvo'
  | 'Exotic & British (Ferrari / Lambo / JLR)';

export type EngineConfigurationType =
  | 'V8'
  | 'V6'
  | 'VR6'
  | 'I6'
  | 'I5'
  | 'I4'
  | 'I3'
  | 'H4'
  | 'H6'
  | 'V10'
  | 'V12'
  | 'W12'
  | 'Rotary';

export type EngineFuelType = 'Gasoline' | 'Diesel' | 'Hybrid / Gasoline';

export type EngineAspirationType =
  | 'Naturally Aspirated'
  | 'Turbocharged'
  | 'Twin-Turbo'
  | 'Supercharged';

export interface EngineCamBaseline {
  camProfileId: CamProfileId;
  name: string;
  lifterType: string;
  intakeDur050: number;
  exhDur050: number;
  lobeLift: number;
  rockerRatio: number;
  valveLiftIntake: number;
  valveLiftExhaust: number;
  lsa: number;
  ivcDeg: number;
  baseHp: number;
  baseTq: number;
  hpRpm: number;
  tqRpm: number;
  ve: number;
  ratingStandard: 'SAE Gross' | 'SAE Net' | 'Factory / Ray Baseline';
  isEstimated: boolean;
  notes: string;
}

export interface EngineTransmissionMatch {
  transId: string; // Must match a key in TRANSMISSIONS_DB (src/data/engineData.ts)
  fitment: 'direct' | 'variant_bellhousing' | 'adapter';
  notes: string;
}

export interface EngineFamilySpec {
  id: string;
  brand: EngineBrand;
  make: string;
  family: string;
  code: string;
  name: string;
  shortName: string;
  configuration: EngineConfigurationType;
  cylinders: number;
  yearStart: number;
  yearEnd: number;
  bore: number;            // inches
  stroke: number;          // inches
  displacementCi: number;  // factory advertised / calculated CID
  displacementL: number;   // liters
  rodLength: number;       // inches
  deckHeight: number;      // inches
  deckClearance: number;   // inches (negative = piston proud of deck)
  gasketThickness: number; // inches
  chamberCc: number;       // cc
  pistonReliefCc: number;  // cc (positive = dish/relief, negative = dome)
  factoryCR: string;       // published factory compression ratio range
  blockMaterial: string;
  headType: string;
  firingOrder: string;
  bellhousingPattern: string;
  bellhousingNotes: string;
  recommendedTransId: string; // Must match TRANSMISSIONS_DB
  compatibleTransmissions: EngineTransmissionMatch[];
  camBaseline: EngineCamBaseline;
  inductionBaseline: {
    selectedIntake: string;
    inductionDress: InductionDressMode;
    carbCfm: number;
    targetAFR: number;
    ignitionTiming: number;
    sparkPlugGap: number;
    fuelSystemLabel: string;
  };
  pistonAlloyBaseline: PistonAlloy;
  ringApplicationBaseline: RingApplication;
  fuelType?: EngineFuelType;
  aspiration?: EngineAspirationType;
  estimatedFields: string[];
  shopNotes: string;
}

/**
 * Multi-brand factory engine family specification database following the
 * TRANSMISSIONS_DB record pattern. Values without a single uniform factory
 * blueprint spec (such as exact .050" cam duration on vintage/VVT engines or
 * replacement composition gasket thickness) are explicitly flagged in
 * `estimatedFields` and `camBaseline.isEstimated`.
 */

export const ENGINE_FAMILY_LIST: EngineFamilySpec[] = Object.values(ENGINE_FAMILIES);

export const ENGINE_BRANDS: EngineBrand[] = [
  'GM / Chevrolet',
  'Pontiac / Oldsmobile / Buick',
  'Ford',
  'Chrysler / Mopar',
  'Jeep / AMC',
  'Toyota / Lexus',
  'Honda / Acura',
  'Nissan / Infiniti',
  'Subaru',
  'Mazda',
  'Mitsubishi',
  'Hyundai / Kia / Genesis',
  'BMW / MINI',
  'Mercedes-Benz / AMG',
  'Volkswagen / Audi / Porsche',
  'Volvo',
  'Exotic & British (Ferrari / Lambo / JLR)'
];

export const ENGINE_CONFIGURATIONS: EngineConfigurationType[] = [
  'V8',
  'V6',
  'VR6',
  'I6',
  'I5',
  'I4',
  'I3',
  'H4',
  'H6',
  'V10',
  'V12',
  'W12',
  'Rotary'
];

export function getEngineFamilyById(id?: string): EngineFamilySpec | undefined {
  if (!id) return undefined;
  return ENGINE_FAMILIES[id];
}

export function getEngineFamiliesByBrand(brand: EngineBrand): EngineFamilySpec[] {
  return ENGINE_FAMILY_LIST.filter((f) => f.brand === brand);
}

export function getEngineFamiliesByConfiguration(
  configType: EngineConfigurationType
): EngineFamilySpec[] {
  return ENGINE_FAMILY_LIST.filter((f) => f.configuration === configType);
}

/**
 * @license
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

import { EngineBrand, ENGINE_BRANDS, ENGINE_FAMILIES } from './engineFamilies';
import { VEHICLE_CATALOG_GLOBAL } from './vehicleCatalogGlobal';

export interface VehicleCatalogYearOption {
  yearStart: number;
  yearEnd: number;
  engineFamilyIds: string[]; // Strictly references keys in ENGINE_FAMILIES
  trimNote?: string;
}

export interface VehicleCatalogEntry {
  id: string;
  brand: EngineBrand;
  make: string;
  model: string;
  chassisCode: string;
  yearStart: number;
  yearEnd: number;
  category: 'Muscle Car' | 'Pickup / Work Truck' | 'SUV / 4x4' | 'Sports Car' | 'Sedan / Fleet';
  tunnelSize: string;
  yearEngineOptions: VehicleCatalogYearOption[];
  notes?: string;
}

/**
 * Extensible US shop vehicle catalog (~1965–2026).
 * Every engine option strictly points by ID to ENGINE_FAMILIES (zero duplicated specs).
 * Add new makes/models by appending entries following this exact schema.
 */
export const VEHICLE_CATALOG: Record<string, VehicleCatalogEntry> = {

};

export const VEHICLE_CATALOG_LIST: VehicleCatalogEntry[] = Object.values(VEHICLE_CATALOG);

export function getCatalogBrands(): EngineBrand[] {
  return ENGINE_BRANDS.filter((brand) =>
    VEHICLE_CATALOG_LIST.some((v) => v.brand === brand)
  );
}

export function getVehiclesForBrand(brand: EngineBrand): VehicleCatalogEntry[] {
  return VEHICLE_CATALOG_LIST.filter((v) => v.brand === brand);
}

export function getValidYearsForVehicle(vehicleId: string): number[] {
  const entry = VEHICLE_CATALOG[vehicleId];
  if (!entry) return [];
  const years = new Set<number>();
  entry.yearEngineOptions.forEach((opt) => {
    for (let y = opt.yearStart; y <= opt.yearEnd; y++) {
      years.add(y);
    }
  });
  return Array.from(years).sort((a, b) => b - a);
}

export function getEnginesForVehicleYear(
  vehicleId: string,
  year: number
): { engineFamilyIds: string[]; trimNotes: string[] } {
  const entry = VEHICLE_CATALOG[vehicleId];
  if (!entry) return { engineFamilyIds: [], trimNotes: [] };

  const idSet = new Set<string>();
  const trimNotes: string[] = [];

  entry.yearEngineOptions.forEach((opt) => {
    if (year >= opt.yearStart && year <= opt.yearEnd) {
      opt.engineFamilyIds.forEach((id) => {
        if (ENGINE_FAMILIES[id]) {
          idSet.add(id);
        }
      });
      if (opt.trimNote) {
        trimNotes.push(opt.trimNote);
      }
    }
  });

  return {
    engineFamilyIds: Array.from(idSet),
    trimNotes
  };
}

const TIER_PRICE_MULT: Record<PartsGradeTier, number> = {
  oe_standard: 1.0,
  pro_hd: 1.24,
  street_perf: 1.55,
  race_forged: 2.35
};

/**
 * Generates authentic professional auto-parts-counter SKUs tailored to
 * the chosen engine family, cylinder count, and requested parts grade tier.
 */
export function getStorePartsForEngine(
  engine: StoreEngineSpec,
  tier: PartsGradeTier
): StorePartOption[] {
  const m = TIER_PRICE_MULT[tier];
  const cylFactor = engine.cylinders / 8;
  const p = engine.partsPrefix;
  const boreStr = `${engine.bore.toFixed(3)}"`;

  const brandByTier = {
    oe_standard: {
      kit: 'Mahle Original / Clevite',
      piston: 'Sealed Power Cast',
      bearing: 'Clevite P-Series OE',
      gasket: 'Fel-Pro PermaTorque',
      oilPump: 'Melling Stock Volume',
      timing: 'Cloyes OEM Timing Set',
      cam: 'Melling OE Stock Cam',
      waterPump: 'ACDelco Gold / Gates',
      ignition: 'ACDelco Iridium / OEM Coil',
      fasteners: 'Fel-Pro ES Torque-to-Yield'
    },
    pro_hd: {
      kit: 'Federal-Mogul Commercial Pro',
      piston: 'Mahle PowerPak Pro',
      bearing: 'Clevite P-Series Tri-Metal',
      gasket: 'Fel-Pro Severe Duty',
      oilPump: 'Melling High-Volume HV',
      timing: 'Cloyes Heavy-Duty Double Roller',
      cam: 'Comp Cams High-Energy Towing',
      waterPump: 'Gates FleetRunner HD',
      ignition: 'NGK Laser Iridium + Standard Blue Streak',
      fasteners: 'ARP Pro-Series Hex Bolt Kit'
    },
    street_perf: {
      kit: 'Scat / Keith Black Master Street Kit',
      piston: 'Keith Black Hypereutectic',
      bearing: 'Clevite H-Series Narrowed',
      gasket: 'Fel-Pro Performance 1003',
      oilPump: 'Melling Select High-Volume',
      timing: 'Cloyes True-Roller 9-Keyway',
      cam: 'Comp Cams Xtreme Energy Roller',
      waterPump: 'Edelbrock Victor Aluminum',
      ignition: 'MSD Pro-Billet / Blaster Coil',
      fasteners: 'ARP High-Performance 12-Pt Kit'
    },
    race_forged: {
      kit: 'Callies 4340 / Wiseco Forged Pro Kit',
      piston: 'Wiseco 2618 Forged ArmorGlide',
      bearing: 'Clevite H-Series Coated Race',
      gasket: 'Cometic Multi-Layer Steel (MLS)',
      oilPump: 'Melling Billet Shark-Tooth Race',
      timing: 'Jesel / Cloyes Hex-A-Just Billet',
      cam: 'Comp Cams Solid Roller Custom Grind',
      waterPump: 'Meziere 55 GPM Electric Billet',
      ignition: 'MSD Digital 6AL + Pro-Billet Race',
      fasteners: 'ARP 2000 / Custom Age 625+ Stud Kit'
    }
  }[tier];

  const warrantyByTier: Record<PartsGradeTier, string> = {
    oe_standard: '24 Mo / 24,000 Mi',
    pro_hd: '36 Mo Commercial Unlimited',
    street_perf: '12 Mo Performance Limited',
    race_forged: 'Competition Race Spec'
  };

  const stockLabel =
    tier === 'oe_standard' || tier === 'pro_hd'
      ? 'IN STOCK — HUB COUNTER #1 (Same-Day Pickup)'
      : tier === 'street_perf'
      ? 'IN STOCK — PRO PERFORMANCE BAY'
      : 'SHIPS TODAY — SPEED SHOP WAREHOUSE';

  const price = (base: number) => Math.round(base * m * (0.55 + 0.45 * cylFactor) * 100) / 100;

  return [
    {
      id: `${p}_${tier}_masterkit`,
      category: 'Master Engine Rebuild Kit',
      brand: brandByTier.kit,
      sku: `${p}-MK-${tier.toUpperCase().slice(0, 3)}01`,
      name: `${engine.liters.toFixed(1)}L (${engine.cid} CID) Complete Rebuild Kit`,
      tier,
      price: price(895),
      warranty: warrantyByTier[tier],
      specSummary: `${engine.cylinders}-cyl matched kit: pistons (${boreStr}), moly rings, rod/main/cam bearings, full gasket set, oil pump, freeze plugs.`,
      inStock: stockLabel
    },
    {
      id: `${p}_${tier}_pistons`,
      category: 'Pistons & Ring Pack',
      brand: brandByTier.piston,
      sku: `${p}-PST-${Math.round(engine.bore * 1000)}`,
      name: `${brandByTier.piston} ${boreStr} Bore Piston & Ring Set`,
      tier,
      price: price(365),
      warranty: warrantyByTier[tier],
      specSummary: `${boreStr} bore × ${engine.stroke.toFixed(3)}" stroke, ${engine.pistonReliefCc}cc valve relief, precision pin-fit tolerance.`,
      inStock: stockLabel
    },
    {
      id: `${p}_${tier}_bearings`,
      category: 'Rod & Main Bearings',
      brand: brandByTier.bearing,
      sku: `${p}-BRG-MS${tier === 'race_forged' ? 'H' : 'P'}`,
      name: `${brandByTier.bearing} Rod & Main Bearing Set (Std)`,
      tier,
      price: price(118),
      warranty: warrantyByTier[tier],
      specSummary: `TM-77 tri-metal copper-lead alloy, high-eccentricity oil film wedge for 0.0022"–0.0028" journal clearance.`,
      inStock: stockLabel
    },
    {
      id: `${p}_${tier}_gasket`,
      category: 'Head & Block Gaskets',
      brand: brandByTier.gasket,
      sku: `${p}-GSK-${Math.round(engine.gasketThickness * 1000)}`,
      name: `${brandByTier.gasket} ${engine.gasketThickness.toFixed(3)}" Head Gasket Set`,
      tier,
      price: price(112),
      warranty: warrantyByTier[tier],
      specSummary: `Compressed ${engine.gasketThickness.toFixed(3)}" fire-ring seal with Viton valve stem seals & intake/exhaust gaskets.`,
      inStock: stockLabel
    },
    {
      id: `${p}_${tier}_oilpump`,
      category: 'Lubrication / Oil Pump',
      brand: brandByTier.oilPump,
      sku: `${p}-OIL-${tier === 'oe_standard' ? 'M55' : 'M55HV'}`,
      name: `${brandByTier.oilPump} Oil Pump & Welded Pickup`,
      tier,
      price: price(79),
      warranty: warrantyByTier[tier],
      specSummary: `CNC-ground gears with bypass relief spring calibrated for 55–70 PSI hot oil pressure.`,
      inStock: stockLabel
    },
    {
      id: `${p}_${tier}_timing`,
      category: 'Timing Drive Kit',
      brand: brandByTier.timing,
      sku: `${p}-TMG-9300`,
      name: `${brandByTier.timing} Timing Chain / Gear Set`,
      tier,
      price: price(96),
      warranty: warrantyByTier[tier],
      specSummary: `Billet induction-hardened crank sprocket, pre-stretched seamless roller drive, ±4° crank indexing.`,
      inStock: stockLabel
    },
    {
      id: `${p}_${tier}_cam`,
      category: 'Camshaft & Lifters',
      brand: brandByTier.cam,
      sku: `${p}-CAM-224R`,
      name: `${brandByTier.cam} Cam & Lifter Matched Package`,
      tier,
      price: price(410),
      warranty: warrantyByTier[tier],
      specSummary: `Matched to ${engine.configLabel} ${engine.firingOrder} firing order with compatible valve spring pressure spec.`,
      inStock: stockLabel
    },
    {
      id: `${p}_${tier}_waterpump`,
      category: 'Cooling / Water Pump',
      brand: brandByTier.waterPump,
      sku: `${p}-WP-8810`,
      name: `${brandByTier.waterPump} High-Flow Water Pump`,
      tier,
      price: price(115),
      coreCharge: tier === 'oe_standard' ? 15 : 0,
      warranty: warrantyByTier[tier],
      specSummary: `Equalized coolant flow impeller with heavy-duty roller ball bearings & Viton mechanical seal.`,
      inStock: stockLabel
    },
    {
      id: `${p}_${tier}_ignition`,
